**Investigating trapdoor mechanics**

I'm currently dissecting the Mineflayer and Prismarine-block APIs to understand trapdoor behavior in Minecraft. My focus is on how to programmatically interact with them, and I'm exploring the creation of an example bot that can respond to chat commands for trapdoor manipulation.

**Logging trapdoor states**

I'm adding `console.log` statements within `ActivateTrapdoor` in `src/trapdor.js` to thoroughly inspect the `trapdoor` object's properties. My goal is to precisely determine its current open/closed state before triggering activation, ensuring predictable bot behavior.

**Refining trapdoor activation**

I'm refining the `DeactivateTrapdoor` function, ensuring accurate traversal to the trapdoor's vicinity and precise aiming. I'm also logging the block object to ascertain the trapdoor's current state, aiming for more robust activation logic before initiating the `activateBlock` command.

```json
}
```

**Tool: bash**

```json
{
  "command": "node -e \"\nconst { Vec3 } = require('vec3');\nfunction getBehind(facing, coord) {\n  const behind = coord.clone();\n  behind.y += 1;\n  switch (facing) {\n    case 'north': behind.z += 1; break;\n    case 'south': behind.z -= 1; break;\n    case 'west': behind.x += 1; break;\n    case 'east': behind.x -= 1; break;\n  }\n  return behind;\n}\n\nconst pos = new Vec3(10, 64, 10);\nconsole.log('north:', getBehind('north', pos));\nconsole.log('south:', getBehind('south', pos));\nconsole.log('west:', getBehind('west', pos));\nconsole.log('east:', getBehind('east', pos));\n\""
}
```

```json
  "content": "import test from 'node:test';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport minecraft_data from 'minecraft-data';\nimport prismarine_block from 'prismarine-block';\nimport { Vec3 } from 'vec3';\nimport {\n  isTrapdoor,\n  getTrapdoorProperties,\n  isTrapdoorOpen,\n  getBehindTrapdoor\n} from '../src/trapdor.js';\n\nconst mcData = minecraft_data('1.19.4');\nconst Block = prismarine_block('1.19.4');\n\ntest('isTrapdoor correctly identifies trapdoor blocks', () => {\n  const oakTrapdoorStateId = mcData.blocksByName['oak_trapdoor'].defaultState;\n  const stoneStateId = mcData.blocksByName['stone'].defaultState;\n\n  const trapdoorBlock = Block.fromStateId(oakTrapdoorStateId, 0);\n  const stoneBlock = Block.fromStateId(stoneStateId, 0);\n\n  assert.equal(isTrapdoor(trapdoorBlock), true);\n  assert.equal(isTrapdoor(stoneBlock), false);\n  assert.equal(isTrapdoor(null), false);\n  assert.equal(isTrapdoor(undefined), false);\n});\n\ntest('getTrapdoorProperties extracts properties correctly', () => {\n  // Find a state with known facing and open value\n  for (let stateId = mcData.blocksByName['oak_trapdoor'].minStateId; stateId <= mcData.blocksByName['oak_trapdoor'].maxStateId; stateId++) {\n    const block = Block.fromStateId(stateId, 0);\n    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(block);\n    assert.ok(props);\n    assert.ok(['north', 'south', 'east', 'west'].includes(props.facing));\n    assert.ok(typeof props.open === 'boolean');\n    assert.ok(['top', 'bottom'].includes(props.half));\n    assert.equal(isTrapdoorOpen(block), props.open);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('getBehindTrapdoor calculates correct behind coordinates for all facings', () => {\n  const mockBot = {\n    blocks: new Map(),\n    blockAt(pos) {\n      const key = `${pos.x},${pos.y},${pos.z}`;\n      return this.blocks.get(key) || null;\n    }\n  };\n\n  const trapdoorCoord = new Vec3(10, 64, 20);\n\n  // Helper to create a trapdoor block with specific facing\n  function createTrapdoorWithFacing(facing) {\n    for (let stateId = mcData.blocksByName['oak_trapdoor'].minStateId; stateId <= mcData.blocksByName['oak_trapdoor'].maxStateId; stateId++) {\n      const b = Block.fromStateId(stateId, 0);\n      if (b.getProperties().facing === facing) {\n        return b;\n      }\n    }\n    throw new Error('State not found for facing ' + facing);\n  }\n\n  // Test North: opens to north (-Z) -> supporting block is at south (+Z) -> (10, 65, 21)\n  mockBot.blocks.set('10,64,20', createTrapdoorWithFacing('north'));\n  const behindNorth = getBehindTrapdoor(mockBot, trapdoorCoord);\n  assert.deepEqual(behindNorth, new Vec3(10, 65, 21));\n\n  // Test South: opens to south (+Z) -> supporting block is at north (-Z) -> (10, 65, 19)\n  mockBot.blocks.set('10,64,20', createTrapdoorWithFacing('south'));\n  const behindSouth = getBehindTrapdoor(mockBot, trapdoorCoord);\n  assert.deepEqual(behindSouth, new Vec3(10, 65, 19));\n\n  // Test West: opens to west (-X) -> supporting block is at east (+X) -> (11, 65, 20)\n  mockBot.blocks.set('10,64,20', createTrapdoorWithFacing('west'));\n  const behindWest = getBehindTrapdoor(mockBot, trapdoorCoord);\n  assert.deepEqual(behindWest, new Vec3(11, 65, 20));\n\n  // Test East: opens to east (+X) -> supporting block is at west (-X) -> (9, 65, 20)\n  mockBot.blocks.set('10,64,20', createTrapdoorWithFacing('east'));\n  const behindEast = getBehindTrapdoor(mockBot, trapdoorCoord);\n  assert.deepEqual(behindEast, new Vec3(9, 65, 20));\n});\n"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "content": "import mineflayer_pathfinder from \"mineflayer-pathfinder\";\nimport { Vec3 } from \"vec3\";\nimport sleep from \"./sleep.js\";\n\nconst { goals } = mineflayer_pathfinder;\n\n/**\n * Checks if a given block is any kind of trapdoor.\n * @param {import(\"prismarine-block\").Block | null | undefined} block\n * @returns {boolean}\n */\nexport function isTrapdoor(block) {\n    if (!block || typeof block.name !== \"string\") return false;\n    return block.name.endsWith(\"_trapdoor\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns block state properties of a trapdoor (facing, half, open, powered, waterlogged).\n * @param {import(\"prismarine-block\").Block | null | undefined} block\n * @returns {{ facing: \"north\" | \"south\" | \"east\" | \"west\", half: \"top\" | \"bottom\", open: boolean, powered: boolean, waterlogged: boolean } | null}\n */\nexport function getTrapdoorProperties(block) {\n    if (!block || !isTrapdoor(block)) return null;\n    if (typeof block.getProperties === \"function\") {\n        return block.getProperties();\n    }\n    return block._properties || null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns true if the trapdoor is currently in an open state (vertical).\n * @param {import(\"prismarine-block\").Block | null | undefined} block\n * @returns {boolean}\n */\nexport function isTrapdoorOpen(block) {\n    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(block);\n    return props ? Boolean(props.open) : false;\n}\n\n/**\n * Calculates the standing position on top of the supporting block behind the trapdoor.\n *\n * In Minecraft blockstate logic:\n * - facing=\"north\": Opens toward North (-Z) -> hinge is attached to the block to the South (+Z).\n * - facing=\"south\": Opens toward South (+Z) -> hinge is attached to the block to the North (-Z).\n * - facing=\"west\":  Opens toward West (-X)  -> hinge is attached to the block to the East (+X).\n * - facing=\"east\":  Opens toward East (+X)  -> hinge is attached to the block to the West (-X).\n *\n * @param {import(\"mineflayer\").Bot} bot\n * @param {import(\"vec3\").Vec3} trapdoor_coord\n * @returns {import(\"vec3\").Vec3}\n */\nexport function getBehindTrapdoor(bot, trapdoor_coord) {\n    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3\n        ? trapdoor_coord.floored()\n        : new Vec3(trapdoor_coord.x, trapdoor_coord.y, trapdoor_coord.z).floored();\n\n    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);\n    if (!trapdoor) {\n        throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not loaded in world.`);\n    }\n\n    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor);\n    if (!props || !props.facing) {\n        throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not a trapdoor (name: ${trapdoor.name}).`);\n    }\n\n    const final_position = coord.clone();\n    final_position.y += 1; // Stand on top of the supporting block\n\n    switch (props.facing) {\n        case \"north\":\n            final_position.z += 1;\n            break;\n        case \"south\":\n            final_position.z -= 1;\n            break;\n        case \"west\":\n            final_position.x += 1;\n            break;\n        case \"east\":\n            final_position.x -= 1;\n            break;\n        default:\n            console.warn(`[Trapdoor] Unknown facing value: ${props.facing}`);\n    }\n\n    return final_position;\n}\n\n/**\n * Moves to the trapdoor and ensures it is OPEN (vertical).\n * @param {import(\"mineflayer\").Bot} bot\n * @param {import(\"vec3\").Vec3} trapdoor_coord\n */\nexport async function OpenTrapdoor(bot, trapdoor_coord) {\n    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3\n        ? trapdoor_coord.floored()\n        : new Vec3(trapdoor_coord.x, trapdoor_coord.y, trapdoor_coord.z).floored();\n\n    if (bot.pathfinder) {\n        await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalNear(coord.x, coord.y, coord.z, 2.5));\n    }\n\n    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));\n\n    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);\n    if (!trapdoor) throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not loaded.`);\n\n    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor);\n    console.log(`[Trapdoor] OpenTrapdoor state at ${coord}:`, props);\n\n    if (props && !props.open) {\n        console.log(`[Trapdoor] Trapdoor is closed. Activating to OPEN...`);\n        await bot.activateBlock(trapdoor);\n        await sleep(250);\n    } else if (props && props.open) {\n        console.log(`[Trapdoor] Trapdoor is already open.`);\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Moves to the trapdoor and ensures it is CLOSED (horizontal).\n * @param {import(\"mineflayer\").Bot} bot\n * @param {import(\"vec3\").Vec3} trapdoor_coord\n */\nexport async function CloseTrapdoor(bot, trapdoor_coord) {\n    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3\n        ? trapdoor_coord.floored()\n        : new Vec3(trapdoor_coord.x, trapdoor_coord.y, trapdoor_coord.z).floored();\n\n    if (bot.pathfinder) {\n        await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalNear(coord.x, coord.y, coord.z, 2.5));\n    }\n\n    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));\n\n    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);\n    if (!trapdoor) throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not loaded.`);\n\n    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor);\n    console.log(`[Trapdoor] CloseTrapdoor state at ${coord}:`, props);\n\n    if (props && props.open) {\n        console.log(`[Trapdoor] Trapdoor is open. Activating to CLOSE...`);\n        await bot.activateBlock(trapdoor);\n        await sleep(250);\n    } else if (props && !props.open) {\n        console.log(`[Trapdoor] Trapdoor is already closed.`);\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Toggles the trapdoor state (open <-> closed).\n * @param {import(\"mineflayer\").Bot} bot\n * @param {import(\"vec3\").Vec3} trapdoor_coord\n */\nexport async function ToggleTrapdoor(bot, trapdoor_coord) {\n    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3\n        ? trapdoor_coord.floored()\n        : new Vec3(trapdoor_coord.x, trapdoor_coord.y, trapdoor_coord.z).floored();\n\n    if (bot.pathfinder) {\n        await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalNear(coord.x, coord.y, coord.z, 2.5));\n    }\n\n    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));\n\n    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);\n    if (!trapdoor) throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not loaded.`);\n\n    console.log(`[Trapdoor] Toggling trapdoor at ${coord}:`, getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor));\n    await bot.activateBlock(trapdoor);\n    await sleep(250);\n}\n\n/**\n * Stasis Chamber sequence:\n * 1. Checks and equips an ender pearl.\n * 2. Ensures the trapdoor is OPEN so the pearl can enter the stasis water column.\n * 3. Navigates behind the trapdoor onto the supporting block.\n * 4. Aims straight down into the stasis chamber.\n * 5. Sneaks towards the edge and throws the ender pearl.\n * \n * @param {import(\"mineflayer\").Bot} bot\n * @param {import(\"vec3\").Vec3} trapdoor_coord \n */\nexport async function SetupEnderPearl(bot, trapdoor_coord) {\n    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3\n        ? trapdoor_coord.floored()\n        : new Vec3(trapdoor_coord.x, trapdoor_coord.y, trapdoor_coord.z).floored();\n\n    const pearlItem = bot.inventory.items().find(item => item.name === \"ender_pearl\");\n    if (!pearlItem) {\n        console.warn(\"[Trapdoor] No ender pearl in inventory!\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    console.log(`[Trapdoor] Setting up ender pearl at ${coord}...`);\n\n    // 1. Ensure trapdoor is open\n    await OpenTrapdoor(bot, coord);\n\n    // 2. Equip ender pearl\n    await bot.equip(pearlItem, \"hand\");\n    console.log(\"[Trapdoor] Equipped ender pearl.\");\n\n    // 3. Move behind the trapdoor onto the block it's attached to\n    const behindPos = getBehindTrapdoor(bot, coord);\n    console.log(`[Trapdoor] Moving behind trapdoor to ${behindPos}...`);\n    if (bot.pathfinder) {\n        await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalBlock(behindPos.x, behindPos.y, behindPos.z));\n    }\n\n    // 4. Look towards the trapdoor block center\n    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));\n\n    // 5. Look straight down (-PI/2 pitch in Mineflayer) into the water/bubble column\n    await bot.look(bot.entity.yaw, -Math.PI / 2, true);\n    await sleep(300);\n\n    // 6. Sneak forward slightly to align with the edge\n    bot.setControlState(\"sneak\", true);\n    await sleep(300);\n    bot.setControlState(\"forward\", true);\n    await sleep(600);\n    bot.setControlState(\"forward\", false);\n    await sleep(300);\n\n    // 7. Throw the pearl\n    console.log(\"[Trapdoor] Throwing ender pearl...\");\n    bot.activateItem();\n    await sleep(500);\n\n    // 8. Stop sneaking\n    bot.setControlState(\"sneak\", false);\n    console.log(\"[Trapdoor] SetupEnderPearl completed successfully!\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Aliases for compatibility:\nexport const ActivateTrapdoor = CloseTrapdoor;\nexport const DeactivateTrapdoor = OpenTrapdoor;\nexport const GetBehindTrapdoor = getBehindTrapdoor;\n"
}
```

**Reviewing Investigation Findings**

I've confirmed all syntax checks are complete. My current focus is on synthesizing these findings to articulate a clear and concise explanation in Spanish, specifically detailing the mechanics of trapdoors within Minecraft and their implementation via Mineflayer.

**Analyzing Trapdoor Mechanics**

I'm currently delving into the specifics of `prismarine-block` properties. My focus is on understanding `_properties` and `getProperties()`, particularly how `open`, `facing`, and `half` dictate trapdoor behavior and orientation. I'm also examining `powered` and `waterlogged` states for a comprehensive grasp of their interactions.

**Exploring Trapdoor Logic**

I'm currently focusing on how the bot interacts with trapdoors, specifically the right-click action to toggle their open state. I've noted that `block.open` and `block.facing` aren't direct properties, but are retrieved via `getProperties()`. My attention is now on implementing the Ender Pearl Stasis Chamber mechanics.



### 🔍 Investigación sobre las Trapdoors en Minecraft y Mineflayer

---

### 1. Propiedades en Prismarine-Block / Mineflayer

Al obtener un bloque con `bot.blockAt(coord)`, las propiedades del block state no están directamente en la raíz (`trapdoor.open` o `trapdoor.facing` dan `undefined`), sino a través de **`block.getProperties()`** o `block._properties`:

```js
const props = trapdoor.getProperties();
// {
//   facing: "north" | "south" | "east" | "west",
//   half: "top" | "bottom",
//   open: true | false,
//   powered: true | false,
//   waterlogged: true | false
// }
```

* **`open`**: `false` = Cerrada (horizontal), `true` = Abierta (vertical).
* **`facing`**: Dirección hacia la que se abre la trampilla. La bisagra / bloque de soporte está en la dirección contraria:
  * `north`: Se abre hacia el Norte ($-Z$), su soporte está al **Sur ($+Z$)**.
  * `south`: Se abre hacia el Sur ($+Z$), su soporte está al **Norte ($-Z$)**.
  * `west`:  Se abre hacia el Oeste ($-X$), su soporte está al **Este ($+X$)**.
  * `east`:  Se abre hacia el Este ($+X$), su soporte está al **Oeste ($-X$)**.
* **`half`**: `top` (mitad superior del bloque) o `bottom` (mitad inferior).
* **`waterlogged`**: Indica si está sumergida en agua (común en stasis chambers).

---

### 2. Interacción y Stasis Chamber

* **Activar/Alternar**: `await bot.activateBlock(trapdoor)` simula el clic derecho y cambia el estado de `open` en trampillas de madera.
* **Pitch para mirar abajo**: En Mineflayer el pitch va de $-\frac{\pi}{2}$ (recto hacia abajo) a $+\frac{\pi}{2}$ (recto hacia arriba).
* **Flujo Stasis Chamber**:
  1. Abrir trampilla (`open: true`).
  2. Moverse al bloque de soporte detrás de la trampilla (`getBehindTrapdoor`).
  3. Equipar `ender_pearl`.
  4. Agacharse (`sneak`), mirar abajo ($-\frac{\pi}{2}$) y lanzar (`bot.activateItem()`).
  5. Para teletransportar: Cerrar la trampilla (`open: false`), colisionando con la perla suspendida.

---

### 3. Cambios implementados y Bot de Prueba

1. **`src/trapdor.js`**:
   * Limpieza de imports inexistentes (`area.js`, `shulkers.js`).
   * Funciones exportadas: `isTrapdoor`, `getTrapdoorProperties`, `isTrapdoorOpen`, `getBehindTrapdoor`, `OpenTrapdoor`, `CloseTrapdoor`, `ToggleTrapdoor` y `SetupEnderPearl`.
   * Corrección de las 4 orientaciones (`north`, `south`, `east`, `west`) y pitch de lanzamiento.

2. **`src/trapdoor_test_bot.js`** (Bot de pruebas interactivo):
   * Se conecta usando `.env` o valores por defecto.
   * Acepta comandos tanto por el **chat de Minecraft** como directamente desde la **consola / terminal**:
     * `!inspect [x y z]` / `!info`: Muestra todos los datos del bloque, propiedades raw, facing, estado y bloque de soporte.
     * `!open [x y z]`: Abre la trampilla si está cerrada.
     * `!close [x y z]`: Cierra la trampilla si está abierta.
     * `!toggle [x y z]`: Alterna el estado.
     * `!find [radio]`: Busca y lista todas las trapdoors cercanas y sus estados.
     * `!behind [x y z]`: Calcula y muestra la coordenada para posicionarse detrás.
     * `!pearl [x y z]`: Ejecuta la rutina completa de lanzamiento de perla con logs en cada paso.
     * `!goto <x> <y> <z>` y `!come`: Movimiento del bot.
     * `!inventory`: Lista los items y perlas del bot.

3. **Para ejecutar el bot de prueba**:
   ```bash
   npm run trapdoor:test
   ```

Given uncertainty, the ROBUST solution: guarded incremental movement with position feedback:

```
async function sneakToEdge(bot, dir, targetCenterCoord, axis) {
  bot.setControlState('sneak', true);
  while (bot.entity.position[axis] < target - epsilon) {
    bot.setControlState('forward', true);
    await sleep(50); // ~1 tick
    bot.setControlState('forward', false);
    await sleep(20);
    if (!bot.entity.onGround) { abort }
    if (progress stalled too long) abort
  }
}
```

With sneak speed 1.3 b/s: 50ms → 0.065 blocks per burst.
```js
function facingToDirection(facing) {
  switch (facing) {
    case "north": return { axis: "z", dir: -1 };
    case "south": return { axis: "z", dir: +1 };
    case "east":  return { axis: "x", dir: +1 };
    case "west":  return { axis: "x", dir: -1 };
  }
}
```

Guarded walk implementation:

```js
async function guardedSneakWalk(bot, axis, dir, targetCoord, maxCoord, timeoutMs = 8000) {
  bot.setControlState("sneak", true);
  await sleep(200); // let sneak settle
  const start = performance.now? Date.now() : ...
  let lastPos = bot.entity.position[axis];
  let stallCount = 0;
  while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
    const cur = bot.entity.position[axis];
    const dist = (targetCoord - cur) * dir;
    if (dist <= 0.01) break;
    // overshoot guard
    bot.setControlState("forward", true);
    await sleep(40);
    bot.setControlState("forward", false);
    await sleep(60);
    const now = bot.entity.position[axis];
    // check fell
    if (!bot.entity.onGround || bot.entity.velocity.y < -0.1) {
      console.warn(...); bot.setControlState("sneak", false); throw new Error("Bot is falling");
    }
    if (Math.abs(now - cur) < 0.001) stallCount++; else stallCount = 0;
    if (stallCount > 10) throw ...
    if ((maxCoord - now) * dir < 0) { // overshot max
      // step back
      bot.setControlState("back", true); await sleep(40); bot.setControlState("back", false); await sleep(60);
    }
  }
  bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
  bot.setControlState("forward", false);
}
```

```js
  await sleep(2000);

});
bot.on("error"...); bot.on("kicked"...);
```

```js
  const map = {
    east:  { axis: "x", dir: 1,  yaw: -Math.PI / 2 },
    west:  { axis: "x", dir: -1, yaw: Math.PI / 2 },

  };
  return map[facing];
}
```

Implementation detail for precise control: rather than waiting fixed 1500ms, loop:
```js
const startTime = Date.now();
const maxDuration = 2000; // safety
while (Date.now() - startTime < maxDuration) {
    const pos = bot.entity.position;
    const current = axis === 'x' ? pos.x : pos.z;
    const remaining = Math.abs(targetCoord - current);
    if (remaining <= 0.02) break; // reached
    if (!bot.entity.onGround || bot.entity.velocity.y < -0.05) {
        // falling! abort
        bot.setControlState('forward', false);
        throw new Error('Bot started falling while sneaking');
    }
    // keep walking
    bot.setControlState('forward', true);
    await sleep(25);
}
bot.setControlState('forward', false);
```

`Projectile.shootFromRotation` → `shoot(x, y, z, ...)`: the entity is positioned at (x, y, z) passed in. For player-thrown projectiles: `ServerPlayer` throw: `ThrownEnderpearl enderpearl = new ThrownEnderpearl(level, player); enderpearl.shootFromRotation(player, xRot, yRot, 0, 1.5F, 1.0F); level.addFreshEntity(enderpearl);`

And the pearl constructor `ThrownEnderpearl(Level level, LivingEntity owner)`: `super(EntityType.ENDER_PEARL, owner, level)` → `ThrowableProjectile` constructor: `this.moveTo(owner.getX(), owner.getEyeY() - 0.1, owner.getZ(), ...)` → spawns at player X, eyeY - 0.1, player Z. So x = player center x. Then shoot applies velocity from rotation.

For the controlled walk:
- Compute walk direction: horizontal from behind-position block center to trapdoor block center. Axis: 'x' or 'z', sign ±.
- Edge coordinate: the boundary between support block and trapdoor block along that axis. If walking +X: edge = supportX + 1 = trapdoorX = 13.0. Target center: edge + 0.25 (for +X). For -X: edge = supportX = trapdoorX + 1; target = edge - 0.25.
- Start: bot at support center (12.5, 108, 81.5).
- Sneak on. Wait ~200ms for sneak state.
- Loop: check current position along axis. If within 0.03 of target → stop. Else keep forward on, sleep 25ms. Safety timeout 3s. If !onGround or velocity.y < -0.08 → abort (stop forward, throw error / attempt recovery: actually if it starts falling, stop controls; the bot is in water, pathfinder can get out).
- After stopping: wait for velocity to settle (~300ms), verify still onGround.
- Look: face trapdoor for yaw (lookAt trapdoor center), then bot.look(yaw, -π/2, true) → straight down. Hmm wait - which yaw? Straight down throw: yaw irrelevant for trajectory (velocity straight down). But hmm, should I keep looking straight down? The user's working sequence used straight down (after my fix). Keep it.
- Throw: bot.activateItem() - activates held item (pearl) → throws.
- Then: stop sneak, step back? After throwing, the bot can stay hanging (pearl is in stasis). Actually safer: walk back a bit? If the trapdoor later closes and someone teleports... the bot just needs to not fall. Since we stopped at hangover position with sneak off, the bot would FALL (center at edge+0.25 > edge+0.3? no: 13.25 < 13.3 → still grounded without sneak? hitbox 12.95-13.55: corner 12.95 over support ✓ grounded even without sneak). Edge + 0.25 → corner at edge - 0.05 over support → grounded ✓. But to be safe, keep sneak on until we step back: set forward=false, then briefly walk backward (or just turn off sneak - bot remains grounded since corner still over block). Let me now write the improved SetupEnderPearl:

```js
export async function SetupEnderPearl(bot, trapdoor_coord) {
    const coord = floored(trapdoor_coord);

    const pearlItem = bot.inventory.items().find(item => item.name === "ender_pearl");
    if (!pearlItem) { console.warn(...); return false; }

    // 1. open trapdoor
    await OpenTrapdoor(bot, coord);

    // 2. equip pearl
    await bot.equip(pearlItem, "hand");

    // 3. behind position
    const behindPos = getBehindTrapdoor(bot, coord);
    await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalBlock(behindPos.x, behindPos.y, behindPos.z));

    // 4. compute walk axis & edge
    // facing: open panel against support side; bot walks from support block toward trapdoor block
    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(bot.blockAt(coord));
    // direction from support to trapdoor:
    // north → support at z+1 → walk -Z ... wait:
    // facing=north → support south (+Z) → bot at z+1, trapdoor at z → walk -Z (north)
    // facing=south → support north (-Z) → walk +Z
    // facing=west  → support east (+X) → walk -X
    // facing=east  → support west (-X) → walk +X
    const walk = { north:[0,-1], south:[0,1], west:[-1,0], east:[1,0] }[props.facing];

    // edge coordinate along the walk axis:
    // if walking +axis: edge = coord[axis] (trapdoor block min) → e.g. +X: edge x = 13.0
    // if walking -axis: edge = coord[axis] + 1 → e.g. -X: trapdoor x=13 → support x=12, edge=13.0 = coord.x+1
    // target = edge + 0.25 * sign  (hang over 0.25 into the column block)
    const axis = walk[0] !== 0 ? 'x' : 'z';
    const sign = walk[0] !== 0 ? walk[0] : walk[1];
    const edge = sign > 0 ? coord[axis] : coord[axis] + 1;
    const target = edge + 0.25 * sign;

    // 5. look down & sneak-walk with monitoring
    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)); // face the trapdoor (yaw)
    
    bot.setControlState('sneak', true);
    await sleep(300);
    
    const startPos = bot.entity.position.clone();
    const start = axis === 'x' ? startPos.x : startPos.z;
    const distance = Math.abs(target - start);
    console.log(...);
    
    bot.setControlState('forward', true);
    const deadline = Date.now() + 4000;
    let fell = false;
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        await sleep(25);
        const pos = bot.entity.position;
        const cur = axis === 'x' ? pos.x : pos.z;
        if (!bot.entity.onGround || bot.entity.velocity.y < -0.08) { fell = true; break; }
        const remaining = sign > 0 ? target - cur : cur - target;
        if (remaining <= 0.02) break;
    }
    bot.setControlState('forward', false);
    
    if (fell) {
        bot.setControlState('sneak', false);
        throw new Error('Bot fell while sneaking to the edge');
    }
    
    // settle
    await sleep(400);
    
    // verify position
    const finalPos = bot.entity.position;
    const cur = axis === 'x' ? finalPos.x : finalPos.z;
    // check we're within hangover bounds: |cur - target| small and onGround
    
    // 6. throw: keep yaw toward column, pitch slightly forward-down (-80°) hmm or -90°
    await bot.look(bot.entity.yaw, THROW_PITCH, true);
    await sleep(200);
    bot.activateItem();
    
    // 7. cleanup
    await sleep(300);
    bot.setControlState('sneak', false);
}
```

```
136:               surroundingBBs.push(blockBB)
137:             }
138:           }
139:         }
```

```js
export async function SetupEnderPearl(bot, trapdoor_coord, options = {}) {
    const coord = toFlooredVec3(trapdoor_coord);
    const hangover = options.hangover ?? 0.2;
    const log = options.log ?? console.log;

    const pearlItem = bot.inventory.items().find(item => item.name === "ender_pearl");
    if (!pearlItem) { log("[Trapdoor] No ender pearl in inventory!"); return false; }

    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);
    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor);
    if (!props) throw new Error(...);

    await OpenTrapdoor(bot, coord);
    await bot.equip(pearlItem, "hand");

    const behindPos = getBehindTrapdoor(bot, coord);
    await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalBlock(behindPos.x, behindPos.y, behindPos.z));

    // walk direction: from support block toward trapdoor block
    const walkVectors = { north: [0, -1], south: [0, 1], west: [-1, 0], east: [1, 0] };
    const [wx, wz] = walkVectors[props.facing];
    const axis = wx !== 0 ? "x" : "z";
    const sign = wx !== 0 ? wx : wz;
    const edge = sign > 0 ? coord[axis] : coord[axis] + 1;
    const currentPos = bot.entity.position;
    const start = axis === "x" ? currentPos.x : currentPos.z;
    const target = edge + sign * hangover;

    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));

    bot.setControlState("sneak", true);
    await sleep(300);

    bot.setControlState("forward", true);
    const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
    let stopped = false;
    let fell = false;
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        await sleep(25);
        if (!bot.entity.onGround || bot.entity.velocity.y < -0.08) { fell = true; break; }
        const pos = axis === "x" ? bot.entity.position.x : bot.entity.position.z;
        const remaining = sign > 0 ? target - pos : pos - target;
        if (remaining <= 0.02) { stopped = true; break; }
    }
    bot.setControlState("forward", false);

    if (fell) {
        bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
        throw new Error("[Trapdoor] Bot fell while sneaking to the edge!");
    }

    await sleep(400); // settle

    const finalPos = bot.entity.position;
    const achieved = axis === "x" ? finalPos.x : finalPos.z;
    const achievedHangover = (achieved - edge) * sign;
    log(`[Trapdoor] Edge walk done: pos=${finalPos}, hangover=${achievedHangover.toFixed(3)}, stopped=${stopped}, onGround=${bot.entity.onGround}`);

    if (!bot.entity.onGround) { bot.setControlState("sneak", false); throw new Error("[Trapdoor] Bot is not grounded at the edge!"); }

    // throw angle: pearl must clear the open trapdoor panel (0.1875 wide strip on the support side)
    const requiredVx = Math.max(0, 0.3125 + 0.02 - achievedHangover);
    const maxVx = (0.855 - achievedHangover) / 3.5;
    if (requiredVx > maxVx || requiredVx > 0.35) {
        bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
        throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Cannot find safe throw angle (requiredVx=${requiredVx.toFixed(3)}, maxVx=${maxVx.toFixed(3)})`);
    }
    const vx = Math.max(requiredVx, 0.06);
    const pitch = -Math.acos(Math.min(vx / 1.5, 1));

    log(`[Trapdoor] Throw: requiredVx=${requiredVx.toFixed(3)}, using vx=${vx.toFixed(3)}, pitch=${(pitch * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(1)}°`);

    await bot.look(bot.entity.yaw, pitch, true);
    await sleep(200);

    const prePos = bot.entity.position.clone();
    bot.activateItem();
    log("[Trapdoor] Pearl thrown!");
    await sleep(300);

    bot.setControlState("sneak", false);

    const postPos = bot.entity.position;
    const jumped = postPos.distanceTo(prePos) > 1.5;
    if (jumped) { log("[Trapdoor] WARNING: bot teleported right after throwing (pearl hit something nearby)"); return false; }

    return true;
}
```

Implementation of dumpGeometry: 

```js
function blockChar(block) {
    if (!block) return "?";
    if (isTrapdoor(block)) return "T";
    switch (block.name) {
        case "air": case "cave_air": case "void_air": return ".";
        case "water": return "~";
        case "bubble_column": return "B";
        case "soul_sand": return "S";
        case "magma_block": return "M";
        default: return "#";
    }
}

function dumpGeometry(center) {
    console.log("[TEST] Geometry dump (x horizontal, z rows, y levels):");
    const x0 = center.x - 2, x1 = center.x + 2;
    const z0 = center.z - 2, z1 = center.z + 2;
    for (let y = center.y + 2; y >= center.y - 4; y--) {
        const rows = [];
        for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) {
            let row = "";
            for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
                row += blockChar(bot.blockAt(new Vec3(x, y, z)));
            }
            rows.push(row);
        }
        console.log(`  y=${y}  ${rows.join(" | ")}`);
    }
    // details
    for (let y = center.y + 2; y >= center.y - 4; y--) {
        for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) {
            for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
                const b = bot.blockAt(new Vec3(x, y, z));
                if (b && (isTrapdoor(b) || b.name === "soul_sand" || b.name === "water" || b.name === "bubble_column")) {
                    const props = typeof b.getProperties === "function" ? b.getProperties() : "";
                    console.log(`  ${b.name} @ (${x},${y},${z}) ${Object.keys(props).length ? JSON.stringify(props) : ""}`);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Now, code. SetupEnderPearl rewrite:

```js
const LEDGE_TARGET_HANGOVER = 0.37;
const LEDGE_MIN_HANGOVER = 0.34;
const LEDGE_MAX_HANGOVER = 0.43;
const PANEL_WIDTH = 0.1875;
const PEARL_HALF_WIDTH = 0.125;

export async function SetupEnderPearl(bot, trapdoor_coord, options = {}) {
    const coord = trapdoor_coord instanceof Vec3 ? trapdoor_coord.floored() : new Vec3(...).floored();
    const dryRun = options.dryRun === true;
    const log = options.log ?? ((...args) => console.log(...args));

    const pearlItem = bot.inventory.items().find(item => item.name === "ender_pearl");
    if (!pearlItem) { log("[Trapdoor] No ender pearl in inventory!"); return false; }

    const trapdoor = bot.blockAt(coord);
    const props = getTrapdoorProperties(trapdoor);
    if (!props || !props.facing) throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Block at ${coord} is not a trapdoor`);

    await OpenTrapdoor(bot, coord);
    await bot.equip(pearlItem, "hand");

    const behindPos = getBehindTrapdoor(bot, coord);
    await bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalBlock(behindPos.x, behindPos.y, behindPos.z));
    log(`[Trapdoor] Standing behind trapdoor at ${bot.entity.position.floored()}`);

    const walkVectors = { north: [0, -1], south: [0, 1], west: [-1, 0], east: [1, 0] };
    const [wx, wz] = walkVectors[props.facing];
    const axis = wx !== 0 ? "x" : "z";
    const sign = wx !== 0 ? wx : wz;
    const edge = sign > 0 ? coord[axis] : coord[axis] + 1;

    await bot.lookAt(coord.offset(0.5, 0.5, 0.5));

    bot.setControlState("sneak", true);
    await bot.waitForTicks(5);

    const target = edge + sign * LEDGE_TARGET_HANGOVER;
    const startPos = bot.entity.position;
    log(`[Trapdoor] Sneak-walking from (${startPos.x.toFixed(3)}, ${startPos.z.toFixed(3)}) to ${axis}=${target.toFixed(3)} (hangover ${LEDGE_TARGET_HANGOVER})`);

    bot.setControlState("forward", true);
    let reached = false;
    let fell = false;
    for (let tick = 0; tick < 80; tick++) {
        await bot.waitForTicks(1);
        if (!bot.entity.onGround || bot.entity.velocity.y < -0.08) { fell = true; break; }
        const pos = axis === "x" ? bot.entity.position.x : bot.entity.position.z;
        if ((target - pos) * sign <= 0.02) { reached = true; break; }
    }
    bot.setControlState("forward", false);

    if (fell) {
        bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
        throw new Error("[Trapdoor] Bot started falling during the edge walk!");
    }

    await bot.waitForTicks(10);

    const finalPos = bot.entity.position;
    const achieved = ((axis === "x" ? finalPos.x : finalPos.z) - edge) * sign;
    log(`[Trapdoor] Edge walk result: pos=(${finalPos.x.toFixed(3)}, ${finalPos.y.toFixed(3)}, ${finalPos.z.toFixed(3)}), hangover=${achieved.toFixed(3)}, reached=${reached}, onGround=${bot.entity.onGround}`);

    if (!bot.entity.onGround) { bot.setControlState("sneak", false); throw new Error("[Trapdoor] Bot is not grounded at the edge!"); }
    if (achieved < LEDGE_MIN_HANGOVER || achieved > LEDGE_MAX_HANGOVER) {
        bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
        throw new Error(`[Trapdoor] Hangover ${achieved.toFixed(3)} outside safe range [${LEDGE_MIN_HANGOVER}, ${LEDGE_MAX_HANGOVER}], pearl NOT thrown`);
    }

    const pearlClearMargin = achieved - PEARL_HALF_WIDTH - PANEL_WIDTH;
    log(`[Trapdoor] Pearl spawn clearance over open panel: ${pearlClearMargin.toFixed(3)} (must be > 0)`);

    if (dryRun) {
        log("[Trapdoor] Dry run: skipping pearl throw");
        bot.setControlState("sneak", false);
        return true;
    }

    await bot.look(bot.entity.yaw, -Math.PI / 2, true);
    await bot.waitForTicks(4);

    const preThrowPos = bot.entity.position.clone();
    bot.activateItem();
    log("[Trapdoor] Pearl thrown straight down!");
    await bot.waitForTicks(6);
    bot.setControlState("sneak", false);

    const postThrowPos = bot.entity.position;
    if (postThrowPos.distanceTo(preThrowPos) > 1.5) {
        log("[Trapdoor] WARNING: bot teleported right after the throw (pearl probably hit the trapdoor)");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
```

```js
import mineflayer from "mineflayer";
import mineflayer_pathfinder from "mineflayer-pathfinder";
import { Vec3 } from "vec3";
import sleep from "./sleep.js";
import { isTrapdoor, getTrapdoorProperties, OpenTrapdoor, SetupEnderPearl, getBehindTrapdoor } from "./trapdor.js";

const { pathfinder, Movements, goals } = mineflayer_pathfinder;
const DRY_RUN = process.argv.includes("--no-throw");

const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
    host: process.env.SERVER_IP || "localhost",
    port: Number(process.env.SERVER_PORT) || 25565,
    version: process.env.VERSION || "1.19.4",
    username: process.env.USERNAME_TELEPORTER || "TrapdoorTester",
    auth: process.env.AUTH_TELEPORTER || "offline",
    password: process.env.PASSWORD_TELEPORTER || undefined
});

bot.loadPlugin(pathfinder);

bot.on("kicked", reason => console.error("[BOT] Kicked:", reason));
bot.on("error", err => console.error("[BOT] Error:", err));

bot.once("spawn", async () => {
    try {
        await sleep(1500);
        const movements = new Movements(bot);
        movements.canDig = false;
        bot.pathfinder.setMovements(movements);

        console.log(`\n[TEST] Spawned at ${bot.entity.position.floored()}`);

        const trapdoor = bot.findBlock({ matching: b => isTrapdoor(b), maxDistance: 16, count: 1 })[0]...
```

```js
            return;
        }

        console.log("\n[TEST] Monitoring pearl for 8 seconds...");
        const start = Date.now();
        let botTeleported = false;
        let lastBotPos = bot.entity.position.clone();
        let pearlLastSeen = null;
        while (Date.now() - start < 8000) {
            await sleep(500);
            const pearl = Object.values(bot.entities).find(e => e.name === "ender_pearl");
            const botPos = bot.entity.position;
            if (botPos.distanceTo(lastBotPos) > 1.5) { botTeleported = true; }
            lastBotPos = botPos.clone();
            if (pearl) {
                pearlLastSeen = pearl.position.clone();
                console.log(`[TEST] t+${((Date.now()-start)/1000).toFixed(1)}s pearl @ (${pearl.position.x.toFixed(2)}, ${pearl.position.y.toFixed(2)}, ${pearl.position.z.toFixed(2)}) vel=(${pearl.velocity.x.toFixed(2)}, ${pearl.velocity.y.toFixed(2)}, ${pearl.velocity.z.toFixed(2)})`);
            } else {
                console.log(`[TEST] t+${((Date.now()-start)/1000).toFixed(1)}s pearl entity NOT FOUND`);
            }
        }

        console.log(`\n[TEST] Trapdoor final state: ${JSON.stringify(getTrapdoorProperties(bot.blockAt(tdPos)))}`);
        console.log(`[TEST] Bot final position: ${bot.entity.position.floored()} (y should be ${behind.y})`);

        const success = !botTeleported && pearlLastSeen !== null && bot.entity.position.floored().y === behind.y;
        console.log(success ? "\n[TEST] >>> SUCCESS: pearl is in stasis <<<" : "\n[TEST] >>> FAILURE: check the logs above <<<");
        finish(success);
    } catch (err) {
        console.error(`\n[TEST] FAILED: ${err.message}`);
        finish(false);
    }
});

function finish(ok) {
    console.log(`[TEST] Exiting (${ok ? "OK" : "FAIL"})`);
    bot.quit();
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1), 1000);
}

function dumpGeometry(center) { ... }
```
